161: 	base := strings.TrimSuffix(name, ext)

Reasoning
The test in default_node_test.go: `d.parkingNode = clablinks.NewParkingNode(d.Cfg.LongName, "/run/netns/test")` then `d.parkingNode.AddEndpoint(veth(d.parkingNode, iface))`. But wait — the test also calls `d.AdoptEndpoint(veth(d, iface))` for owned endpoints. DefaultNode.AdoptEndpoint — check it exists (it must, since existing test TestDefaultNodeEndpointOwnership uses it). Fine.

But one issue in the test: `veth(d.parkingNode, iface)` — `veth` takes `n clablinks.Node` — is `*ParkingNode` a `clablinks.Node`? ParkingNode implements the Node interface presumably (it's used as link node). NewEndpointGeneric takes `Node`. `d` is `*DefaultNode` — is DefaultNode a `clablinks.Node`? Existing tests use `veth(d, iface)` style? Let me check the existing test at line ~20-50 of default_node_test.go to see how endpoints are created there.

Also `NewEndpointGeneric(n, iface, nil)` — third param `link Link` — passing nil literal works for interface type.

Check existing test usage.

Reasoning
Existing test uses same patterns; the new test compiles similarly. `ParkingNode` implements `links.Node` presumably (it's used as a node in link code, e.g., `captureCandidates(ctx, src Node)` where src is DefaultNode... wait, in ParkEndpoints, `parkingNode.CaptureFrom(ctx, d)` — d is DefaultNode passed as Node. And in topology_reconcile `c.applyParkingLinkNode(nodeName)` returns a parkingNode used as `n`. So ParkingNode satisfies links.Node. Good.

One more check in the new test: `if tt.parked != nil && len(d.Endpoints) > 0 && &got[0] == &d.Endpoints[0]`. In the "all parked" case, owned is nil so `d.Endpoints` is nil — `slices.Clone(nil)` returns nil! Wait: `slices.Clone(d.Endpoints)` with empty/nil slice returns nil (Clone returns nil for empty input — actually `slices.Clone` on empty slice returns empty slice `[]T{}`? Let me recall: `slices.Clone` is `append(S(nil), s...)`, which for nil input returns nil. Then `append(nil, parked...)` allocates. So got = parked endpoints only. `len(d.Endpoints) > 0` false → alias check skipped. Fine.

In "owned and parked split" case: owned = ["eth3"], d.Endpoints has len 1 but grown cap 4 (via slices.Grow). Clone copies → new backing array → no aliasing. Good.

But wait — there's a subtlety in the alias check: `&got[0] == &d.Endpoints[0]` compares addresses of endpoint slice elements. Clone allocates a new array, so addresses differ. OK.

Now, is `slices.Grow(d.Endpoints, 4)` correct? `slices.Grow` returns a slice with at least len+4 capacity — "Spare capacity lets a missing clone show up as aliasing." OK, deliberate.

Test assertion `if len(d.Endpoints) != len(tt.owned)` — for "all parked", tt.owned is nil, len 0; d.Endpoints nil → 0. OK.

Now the docs: check `docs/cmd/save.md` claim vs. c9s runtime — saveWithLabRuntime, does it propagate errors? It was unchanged in this PR. If saveWithLabRuntime swallows errors, the doc's blanket claim "When save fails, it will exit with a non-zero return code" would be inaccurate for c9s. Let me read saveWithLabRuntime fully (lines 392-430).

Let me check moveEndpoint in links. That's important! Let me check how endpoints are transferred between node and parking node.

In `DiscoverOwnedEndpoints` (lines 98-161): it does `p.ReleaseEndpoint(ep)` then `ep.SetNode(original); original.AdoptEndpoint(ep)` for endpoints in p that no longer exist in the netns. Inside it references `errs` and `i`. `errs` is an outer local variable → captured by reference (since the closure outlives the defining iteration and `errs` is reassigned later). Yes.

Actually hold on, one more consideration — could the Go compiler optimize so that the goroutine captures a copy of the slice header at `go` statement time? Go closures capture variables by reference, period (escape analysis may stack-allocate the variable, but sharing semantics remain by-reference). So the goroutine reads the current value of `errs` when it executes `errs[i] = ...`. Race confirmed.

Severity: I'll go with P2 (data race; memory-model violation; would fail `go test -race`; currently value-safe only because the backing array never reallocates given exact capacity, which is a fragile invariant).

## Objective
- Review read-only PR snapshot at /source (containerlab repo) against standards.json rules (karpathy-guidelines.mdc, ponytail.mdc), all 9 changed paths, plus callers/tests/docs/schema; then return a trusted result schema. Source-only review, no test execution — must disclose that limitation.

## Important Details
- Head: 0581db29d40ff5d50b64dce371fb0c0f500406e5; base/target: 91c22792bf11b95fcc34d8b882d745bd3f13083a (mergeBase same). Snapshot complete (`manifest.json` readable).
- 9 changed paths (manifest): M core/save.go; A core/save_test.go; M docs/cmd/save.md; M docs/manual/kinds/cisco_iol.md; M nodes/default_node.go; M nodes/default_node_test.go; M nodes/iol/iol.go; M nodes/linux/linux.go; M nodes/xrd/xrd.go.
- PR theme 1: `CLab.Save` (core/save.go) now collects per-node errors concurrently into `errs` slice and returns `errors.Join(errs...)` instead of logging and returning nil. New test `TestSaveReturnsNodeErrors` in core/save_test.go with `mocks/mocknodes` MockNode.
- PR theme 2: new `DefaultNode.EndpointsWithParked()` (nodes/default_node.go:1115) = `slices.Clone(d.Endpoints)` + `d.parkingNode.GetEndpoints()` when parkingNode != nil; used in `Deploy` CLAB_INTFS (default_node.go:194), linux.go:78, iol.go `ensureNumSlotsEnv` (:231) and `GenInterfaceConfig` (:372), xrd.go `genInterfacesEnv` (:199). ParkingNode API in links/parking_node.go (CaptureFrom/RestoreTo/RepointSymlink; GetEndpoints/AddEndpoint via GenericLinkNode).
- PR theme 3: iol.go `CreateIOLFiles` only creates boot_config.txt if missing (line 343) — fixes truncation on redeploy (GenBootConfig regenerates it in PostDeploy; consistent with nvram FileExists guard). boot_config.txt is bind-mounted as /iol/config.txt (iol.go:167).
- PR theme 4: docs — docs/cmd/save.md documents non-zero exit on save failure (verified: cmd/save.go:31 returns `c.Save(...)` from RunE). docs/manual/kinds/cisco_iol.md adds Telnet tab (port 5000, "press Enter first", warning that console requires image built with srl-labs/vrnetlab after November 2026); tab/admonition nesting verified structurally valid; image claims unverifiable (coverage note).
- Key finding (candidate): **data race in core/save.go** — goroutine closure captures `errs` by reference; `errs[i] = ...` reads the slice header concurrently with main loop's `errs = append(errs, nil)` (no happens-before edge for post-`go`-statement appends). Race detector flaggable; value-benign in practice only because `make([]error, 0, len(c.Nodes))` prevents reallocation (ptr stable). Suggested fix: preallocate `errs := make([]error, len(c.Nodes))` and index without appending. Lean P2 (correctness/memory-model), arguably P3 given practical benignity.
- `i := len(errs)-1` is per-iteration local — no loop-variable capture bug.
- `errors.Join` skips nils; test's `strings.Count(err.Error(), "save failed")` unaffected by "save copy failed" messages (different wording).

No -race in CI workflows found (grep of .github/workflows for "go test|golangci" only shows lint + clabernetes core tests).

So the rule doesn't flag anything; no release note added for user-visible changes. The rule says inspect changes under docs/rn/ — there are none. Should I flag that user-visible behavior changes (save exit code) lack a release note? The rule only mandates checking existing docs/rn/ changes, not adding one. I'll note it in coverage, not a finding.

Now I have everything I need. Let me finalize.

Findings:

1. P2 — Data race on `errs` slice in `CLab.Save` (core/save.go:40-63). Goroutines write `errs[i]` while the main goroutine appends to `errs` — concurrent access to the same slice variable without synchronization. Value-benign in practice (capacity exact, no reallocation), but a memory-model violation that `go test -race` would flag. Fix: preallocate `errs := make([]error, len(c.Nodes))` and assign by index without append (pattern already used in `labruntime/clabernetes/workers.go:45`).

2. P4 — `EndpointsWithParked` returns the live `d.Endpoints` backing array when no parking node is set; no doc comment warns callers not to mutate. Suggest a short doc comment (or always clone). Optional.

Anything else? Let me reconsider the IOL boot_config guard. `CreateIOLFiles` — only creates boot_config.txt if missing. Wait, one more consideration: what if the user intentionally modifies config.txt and wants to reset? Edge case, not a defect.

Let me also double check the diff for default_node.go once more — the import of `slices`. Confirmed in diff (line 198: + "slices").

Is there a missing test for `Save`'s copyDst error path? The new test only covers SaveConfig errors. The old behavior logged copy errors; the new one returns them — no test covers the copy-error propagation. Minor coverage gap — mention in coverage notes rather than a finding (verification check 6: recommend smallest useful check — a test with copyDst pointing to an unwritable path). Actually, I could make this a P4 finding: "no check covers copy-error propagation in Save". Hmm, the ponytail rule says non-trivial logic leaves one runnable check behind. The copy-error path is part of the changed behavior (previously logged-only, now returned). A tiny test addition would cover it. I'll include as P4.

Actually wait — let me re-read the new test to make sure there isn't already a copy test... The test file has only TestSaveReturnsNodeErrors (and the base had no tests for Save? base core/save_test.go — the file is new in this PR, "A core/save_test.go"). So yes, only one test.

Hmm, also: does the new test actually exercise the concurrent path deterministically? With 3 nodes and mocked SaveConfig returning immediately, the race may not trigger deterministically — fine, the test asserts error aggregation, not the race.

---

# Code Review: save error propagation and parked-endpoint accounting

## Summary

This PR makes two behavior changes and one fix: (1) `CLab.Save` now aggregates per-node save errors concurrently and returns them via `errors.Join` instead of logging and returning `nil`, with a new table-driven test in `core/save_test.go`; (2) node kinds that depend on the `CLAB_INTFS` environment variable (default node, `linux`, `cisco_iol`, `xrd`) now count parked endpoints during a parking/recreate cycle via the new `DefaultNode.EndpointsWithParked()` helper, which is verified to be consistent with the park → recreate → restore sequence in `core/lifecycle.go` and with the ownership transfer in `links/endpoint.go` (`moveEndpoint` releases from the source node before adopting into the parking node, so there is no double counting); (3) `CreateIOLFiles` no longer truncates an existing `boot_config.txt`, fixing config loss on redeploy since `GenBootConfig` regenerates the file in `PostDeploy`. The documentation changes (`docs/cmd/save.md` exit-code claim, `docs/manual/kinds/cisco_iol.md` Telnet tab) match the implementation as far as this repository can verify them.

One real defect was found: the concurrent error collection in `CLab.Save` accesses the `errs` slice variable from goroutines while the main goroutine keeps appending to it, which is a data race on the slice header (benign in value only because the backing array is preallocated with exact capacity, but still undefined behavior and `go test -race` flaggable).

## Findings

| Priority | Count | Summary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| P0 | 0 | — |
| P1 | 0 | — |
| P2 | 1 | Data race on the `errs` slice in `CLab.Save` |
| P3 | 0 | — |
| P4 | 2 | `EndpointsWithParked` returns the live slice with no doc comment; no check covers `Save` copy-error propagation |

### [P2] Data race on the `errs` slice in `CLab.Save`

**Location:** [core/save.go](https://github.com/srl-labs/containerlab/blob/0581db29d40ff5d50b64dce371fb0c0f500406e5/core/save.go#L41-L62) (introduced by this PR; the base version had no shared error slice).

**Problem:** The loop launches one goroutine per node. Each goroutine captures the outer `errs` variable by reference and executes `errs[i] = ...` (which reads the current slice header to locate the backing array), while the main goroutine continues the loop and executes `errs = append(errs, nil)`. There is no happens-before edge between a later `append` and an earlier goroutine's read/write of the `errs` variable, so the goroutines and the main loop race on the slice header under the Go memory model. In practice the race is value-benign only because `errs` is created with `make([]error, 0, len(c.Nodes))`, so `append` never reallocates and the backing-array pointer stays stable — but that invariant is implicit, fragile (any future change to the capacity expression reintroduces lost writes), and `go test -race` (including the new `TestSaveReturnsNodeErrors`, which exercises this exact code with three goroutines) will flag it when race mode is enabled anywhere in the pipeline.

**Trigger:** Any `clab save` invocation on the Docker runtime path (two or more nodes); deterministically detectable under `go test -race` in `core`.

**Impact:** Undefined behavior per the Go memory model. Today it manifests only as race-detector reports and lost-error risk if the preallocation ever changes; it is a latent reliability defect rather than an observed failure.

**Correction:** Preallocate by length and assign by index, removing the append entirely:

```go
errs := make([]error, len(c.Nodes))
i := 0
for _, node := range c.Nodes {
    i := i
    i++
    go func(node clabnodes.Node) {
        defer wg.Done()
        ...
 errs[i] = ...
    }(node)
}
```

This is already the established in-repo pattern — `labruntime/clabernetes/workers.go:45` uses exactly `errs := make([]error, len(items))` with indexed assignment from worker goroutines — so the fix also removes the awkward `errs = append(errs, nil); i := len(errs)-1` idiom. (Note: `i` is already a per-iteration local, so there is no loop-variable capture bug; the issue is solely the concurrent slice-header access.)

### [P4] `EndpointsWithParked` returns the live `d.Endpoints` slice with no usage contract

**Location:** [nodes/default_node.go](https://github.com/srl-labs/containerlab/blob/0581db29d40ff5d50b64dce371fb0c0f500406e5/nodes/default_node.go#L1115-L1127).

**Problem:** When `d.parkingNode` is nil (the steady-state case for every normal deploy), the method returns `d.Endpoints` itself, sharing the node's backing array with the caller. When the parking node is set, `slices.Clone` deliberately protects against aliasing (and the new `TestDefaultNodeEndpointsWithParked` asserts this), but the common case has no such protection and the method carries no doc comment stating the contract.

**Trigger:** A future caller that appends to or mutates the returned slice (e.g., appending an endpoint before `AdoptEndpoint`, which grows the shared array and can overwrite the node's state).

**Impact:** None today — all four current callers (`DefaultNode.Deploy`, `linux.Deploy`, `iol.ensureNumSlotsEnv`/`GenInterfaceConfig`, `xrd.genInterfacesEnv`) only read the result. This is a latent API hazard, not a current defect.

**Correction:** Add a short doc comment to `EndpointsWithParked` stating that the returned slice must not be mutated (mirroring the guarantee the parked branch already provides). Optionally, always `slices.Clone` for uniformity — but a comment is the proportionate fix.

### [P4] No check covers the newly propagated copy-out errors in `Save`

**Location:** [core/save.go](https://github.com/srl-labs/containerlab/blob/0581db29d40ff5d50b64dce371fb0c0f500406e5/core/save.go#L57-L63) and [core/save_test.go](https://github.com/srl-labs/containerlab/blob/0581db29d40ff5d50b64dce371fb0c0f500406e5/core/save_test.go#L14-L75).

**Problem:** The PR changes two error paths in `Save`: `SaveConfig` failures (now covered by `TestSaveReturnsNodeErrors`) and `copySavedConfig` failures, which the base version only logged (`core/save.go:58` base) and which are now returned to the caller. The new test exercises only the first path; the second changed behavior is untested.

**Trigger:** `clab save --copy-out <dst>` where the copy fails, e.g., the mock's `Config()` returns `nil` (producing `node config missing`) or the destination cannot be created.

**Impact:** A regression that silently drops or mis-reports copy failures would not be caught. Low risk, since `copySavedConfig` itself is unchanged, but the changed contract is the reason this path matters now.

**Correction:** Add one table case to `TestSaveReturnsNodeErrors` (or a sibling test) that calls `c.Save(context.Background(), WithCopyOut(t.TempDir()))` with a mock whose `SaveConfig` returns a populated `*nodes.SaveConfigResult` and whose `Config()` returns `nil`; assert the joined error contains `node config missing`. This is a ~10-line addition using the existing mock.

## Coverage Notes

- This is a source-only review: no code was executed, so no tests were run and no race or formatting failures were observed directly. Compilation of the new tests was checked statically against the referenced symbols (`links.NewEndpointGeneric`, `links.NewEndpointVeth`, `Node.AdoptEndpoint`/`ReleaseEndpoint`, `links.NewParkingNode`, `mocknodes.NewMockNode`), all of which exist with compatible signatures.
- The CI workflows do not run Go tests with `-race` (only golangci-lint and clabernetes core tests were found), which is why the P2 race is unlikely to surface in current CI; the finding stands on memory-model grounds regardless.
- `docs/manual/kinds/cisco_iol.md` image-level claims (Telnet served on port 5000 on the management address; availability only in `srl-labs/vrnetlab` images built after November 2026) describe behavior of the external `vrnetlab` project and cannot be verified from this repository; the tab/admonition nesting was checked structurally and is valid.
- The release-note rule (`.cursor/rules/release-note-review.mdc`) was checked: no files under `docs/rn/` are touched by this PR, so the rule raises nothing; whether maintainers want a release note for the `save` exit-code change is a maintainer decision, not a defect.
- No topology schema changes are involved (`EndpointsWithParked` and the save behavior are internal APIs), so `schemas/clab.schema.json` was not consulted beyond confirming the PR does not touch it.
- All nine changed paths were inspected, including their base versions; the parking/recreate flow was traced through `core/lifecycle.go`, `core/apply.go`, and `links/parking_node.go` to confirm the parked-endpoint counting is correct and free of double counting, including the failed-restore path where `d.parkingNode` is retained.

```json
{
  "version": 1,
  "findings": [
    {
      "priority": "P2",
      "title": "Data race on the `errs` slice in `CLab.Save`",
      "location": {
        "revision": "head",
        "path": "core/save.go",
        "start": 41,
        "end": 62
      },
      "problem": "The per-node goroutines launched in `CLab.Save` capture the outer `errs` variable by reference and execute `errs[i] = ...` while the main goroutine continues to run `errs = append(errs, nil)`. There is no happens-before edge between a goroutine's access and the main loop's later appends, so the goroutines and the main loop race on the slice header. The race is value-benign today only because `errs` is created with `make([]error, 0, len(c.Nodes))`, so `append` never reallocates the backing array; that invariant is implicit and fragile, and `go test -race` (including the new `TestSaveReturnsNodeErrors`, which spawns three such goroutines) flags it whenever race mode is enabled.",
      "trigger": "Any `clab save` on the Docker runtime path with two or more nodes; detectable by running `go test -race ./core/`, which the CI workflows currently do not do.",
      "impact": "Undefined behavior under the Go memory model. No lost writes occur today because the backing array is never reallocated, but any future change to the capacity expression reintroduces the risk of silently dropping node save errors, and race-detector builds will report the race.",
      "suggestion": "Preallocate by length and assign by index: `errs := make([]error, len(c.Nodes))` with each goroutine writing `errs[i]` for its pre-assigned index and no appends anywhere. This matches the existing in-repo pattern in `labruntime/clabernetes/workers.go:45` (`errs := make([]error, len(items))`) and also removes the awkward `errs = append(errs, nil); i := len(errs)-1` idiom. Note that `i` is already a per-iteration local, so there is no loop-variable capture issue; the defect is solely the concurrent slice-header access."
    },
    {
      "priority": "P4",
      "title": "`EndpointsWithParked` returns the live `d.Endpoints` slice with no usage contract",
      "location": {
        "revision": "head",
        "path": "nodes/default_node.go",
        "start": 1115,
        "end": 1127
      },
      "problem": "When `d.parkingNode` is nil, which is the steady state for every normal deploy, `EndpointsWithParked` returns `d.Endpoints` itself, sharing the node's backing array with the caller. The parked branch deliberately clones (`slices.Clone`, asserted by `TestDefaultNodeEndpointsWithParked`), but the common case has no such protection and the method has no doc comment stating the contract.",
      "trigger": "A future caller that appends to or mutates the returned slice, for example appending an endpoint before `AdoptEndpoint`, which grows the shared backing array and can corrupt node state.",
      "impact": "No current caller mutates the result; all four call sites only read it. This is a latent API hazard, not an active defect.",
      "suggestion": "Add a short doc comment to `EndpointsWithParked` stating that the returned slice must not be mutated, mirroring the aliasing guarantee the parked branch already provides. Optionally always `slices.Clone`, but a comment is the proportionate fix."
    },
    {
      "priority": "P4",
      "title": "No check covers the newly propagated `copySavedConfig` errors in `Save`",
      "location": {
        "revision": "head",
        "path": "core/save_test.go",
        "start": 14,
        "end": 75
      },
      "problem": "This PR changes two error paths in `Save`: `SaveConfig` failures (covered by the new `TestSaveReturnsNodeErrors`) and `copySavedConfig` failures, which the base version only logged and which are now returned to the caller. The new test exercises only the first path, so the second changed contract is untested.",
      "trigger": "Running `clab save --copy-out <dst>` where the copy fails, for example when `node.Config()` returns nil, producing the `node config missing` error from `copySavedConfig`.",
      "impact": "A regression that drops or mis-reports copy failures would go unnoticed. Risk is low because `copySavedConfig` itself is unchanged, but the propagation change is exactly what the PR introduces.",
      "suggestion": "Add one table case to `TestSaveReturnsNodeErrors` (or a sibling test) that calls `c.Save(context.Background(), WithCopyOut(t.TempDir()))` with a mock whose `SaveConfig` returns a populated `*nodes.SaveConfigResult` and whose `Config()` returns nil, then asserts the joined error contains `node config missing`. This is roughly a ten-line addition using the existing mock."
    }
```
